### Mangaluru

#### Private medical colleges bound to provide services free of cost, says Khader

**In short:** Health Minister U.T. Khader expressed strong displeasure over the lackadaisical approach of private medical colleges in providing services at government healthcare centres, saying colleges are bound to provide services free of cost.

Health Minister U.T. Khader expressed strong displeasure over the lackadaisical approach of private medical colleges in providing services at government healthcare centres, saying colleges are bound to provide services free of cost. He told reporters in Mangaluru on Wednesday that if they do not abide by conditions, the State government will cancel the Memorandum of Understanding and inform the National Medical Commission. At a recent review meeting he found colleges not providing doctors and other services required under MoUs meant to fill personnel vacancies and provide specialised treatment at government hospitals; he cited Community Health Centre in Ullal as a fine example where a private institution provided four MBBS doctors, twice-a-week specialist clinics and 10 nurses, while three private medical colleges in Dakshina Kannada signed MoUs for Bantwal Taluk Hospital, Moodbidri CHC and Vitla CHC, and a private hospital is yet to start at Belthangady Taluk Hospital.

**What this is**
- **Concept:** Private medical colleges bound to provide services free of cost, says Khader
- **Told:** Health Minister U.T.
- **Purpose:** At a recent review meeting he found colleges not providing doctors and other services required under MoUs meant to fill personnel vacancies and provide specialised treatment at government hospitals; he cited Community Health Centre in Ullal as a fine example where a private institution provided four MBBS doctors, twice-a-week specialist clinics and 10 nurses, while three private medical colleges in Dakshina Kannada signed MoUs for Bantwal Taluk Hospital, Moodbidri CHC and Vitla CHC, and a private hospital is yet to start at Belthangady Taluk Hospital.

**Important points**
- He told reporters in Mangaluru on Wednesday that if they do not abide by conditions, the State government will cancel the Memorandum of Understanding and inform the National Medical Commission.
- At a recent review meeting he found colleges not providing doctors and other services required under MoUs meant to fill personnel vacancies and provide specialised treatment at government hospitals.
- he cited Community Health Centre in Ullal as a fine example where a private institution provided four MBBS doctors, twice-a-week specialist clinics and 10 nurses, while three private medical colleges in Dakshina Kannada signed MoUs for Bantwal Taluk Hospital.
- Moodbidri CHC and Vitla CHC, and a private hospital is yet to start at Belthangady Taluk Hospital.
_Sources: Deccan Herald Mangaluru p.2; The Hindu Mangaluru p.2_

#### 2 fishing boats capsize, ten people rescued

**In short:** Two wooden fishing trawlers capsized in the estuary near Mangaluru Fisheries Harbour on Tuesday evening but all 10 labourers aboard were rescued without injuries; one boat was towed ashore while the rope pulling the second snapped and it completely capsized. The vessels were Mermaid II, owned by Shareef of Alekala, Ullal, and Aniq, owned by Aboobakkar Azmal of Bengre Kasaba, Kuloor.

#### Schools told to strictly adhere to safety standards for vehicles

**In short:** The Mangaluru City Traffic Police organised an awareness programme on school bus safety at Town Hall for around 600 drivers, owners and school representatives, instructing managements to adhere to Karnataka Motor Vehicles Rules, 2012, verify vehicle and driver documents, ensure fire extinguishers and speed governors, and keep speeds below 40 kmph. DCP (Crime and Traffic) Ravi Shankar K warned that legal action would be taken against violators.

#### DK administration takes steps to include missing voter's name in final electoral roll

**In short:** Admitting an error marking 63-year-old Mangaluru city resident Donald Joseph Rego as dead and removing him from the draft electoral roll, the Dakshina Kannada district administration stated steps have been taken to include his name in the final electoral roll. Additional District Electoral Registration Officer and Additional Deputy Commissioner K.

Admitting an error marking 63-year-old Mangaluru city resident Donald Joseph Rego as dead and removing him from the draft electoral roll, the Dakshina Kannada district administration stated steps have been taken to include his name in the final electoral roll. Additional District Electoral Registration Officer and Additional Deputy Commissioner K. Raju said in a September 1 statement that on August 29 a BLO noticed Rego was erroneously included in the dead list and his name was deleted from the draft roll; the same day the BLO and jurisdictional ERO contacted Rego and Form 6 was submitted via the BLO App. Rego, residing on Bejai New Road and who voted in the 2024 parliament elections, found his name missing from the draft roll checked August 26 and listed as deceased in the ASDD list; after filing Form 6 on his fourth visit Wednesday, Raju said Rego had only given details in the Enumeration Form and failed to give details of himself or progeny in 2002 electoral rolls, attaching only his father's death certificate, leading the BLO to mark "No Mapping case" and during digitalisation Rego was erroneously marked dead; the ERO Mangaluru City South received the BLO explanation and the DEO served notice to the BLO.

**What this is**
- **Concept:** DK administration takes steps to include missing voter's name in final electoral roll
- **Told:** Admitting an error marking 63-year-old Mangaluru city resident Donald Joseph Rego as dead and removing him from the draft electoral roll, the Dakshina Kannada district administration stated steps have been taken to include his name in the final electoral roll.
- **Purpose:** Rego, residing on Bejai New Road and who voted in the 2024 parliament elections, found his name missing from the draft roll checked August 26 and listed as deceased in the ASDD list; after filing Form 6 on his fourth visit Wednesday, Raju said Rego had only given details in the Enumeration Form and failed to give details of himself or progeny in 2002 electoral rolls, attaching only his father's death certificate, leading the BLO to mark "No Mapping case" and during digitalisation Rego was erroneously marked dead; the ERO Mangaluru City South received the BLO explanation and the DEO served notice to the BLO.

**Important points**
- Raju said in a September 1 statement that on August 29 a BLO noticed Rego was erroneously included in the dead list and his name was deleted from the draft roll.
- the same day the BLO and jurisdictional ERO contacted Rego and Form 6 was submitted via the BLO App.
- Rego, residing on Bejai New Road and who voted in the 2024 parliament elections, found his name missing from the draft roll checked August 26 and listed as deceased in the ASDD list.
- after filing Form 6 on his fourth visit Wednesday.
- Raju said Rego had only given details in the Enumeration Form and failed to give details of himself or progeny in 2002 electoral rolls, attaching only his father's death certificate, leading the BLO to mark "No Mapping case" and during digitalisation Rego was erroneously marked dead.
- the ERO Mangaluru City South received the BLO explanation and the DEO served notice to the BLO.
_Sources: Deccan Herald Mangaluru p.2; The Hindu Mangaluru p.2_

#### MCC asks unauthorised street vendors to vacate public places

**In short:** MCC asked street vendors carrying out vending without authorisation to voluntarily vacate public places, warning that if they failed to do so the corporation would evict them using Sections 12 and 18 of the Street Vendors Act, 2014. In a press release, MCC Central Zone Commissioner cited rising vehicle density and complaints that unauthorised street vendors on pavements were causing traffic obstruction and pedestrian problems.

MCC asked street vendors carrying out vending without authorisation to voluntarily vacate public places, warning that if they failed to do so the corporation would evict them using Sections 12 and 18 of the Street Vendors Act, 2014. In a press release, MCC Central Zone Commissioner cited rising vehicle density and complaints that unauthorised street vendors on pavements were causing traffic obstruction and pedestrian problems. Only 667 street vendors with MCC identity cards and vending certificates are permitted; the remaining vendors were told to leave voluntarily.

#### Shroom Sabha to focus on mushroom magic

**In short:** India's first mushroom festival began as a celebration of mushrooms and the mycelial networks beneath our feet, with the three-day Shroom Sabha annual nature, wellness and mushroom festival set amid the lush forests of Varanashi Organic Farms near Mangaluru. The event brings together foragers, artists, scientists, cultivators, cooks, musicians, farmers and mushroom enthusiasts through forest walks, workshops, reishi tea ceremonies, discussions on biodiversity and biomaterials, live art, music and food.

India's first mushroom festival began as a celebration of mushrooms and the mycelial networks beneath our feet, with the three-day Shroom Sabha annual nature, wellness and mushroom festival set amid the lush forests of Varanashi Organic Farms near Mangaluru. The event brings together foragers, artists, scientists, cultivators, cooks, musicians, farmers and mushroom enthusiasts through forest walks, workshops, reishi tea ceremonies, discussions on biodiversity and biomaterials, live art, music and food. Organisers describe it as a movement to foster fungal literacy and reconnect people with nature, featuring sessions on healing the self, the community and the ecology.

**What this is**
- **Concept:** Shroom Sabha to focus on mushroom magic
- **Told:** India's first mushroom festival began as a celebration of mushrooms and the mycelial networks beneath our feet, with the three-day Shroom Sabha annual nature, wellness and mushroom festival set amid the lush forests of Varanashi Organic Farms near Mangaluru.
- **Purpose:** Organisers describe it as a movement to foster fungal literacy and reconnect people with nature, featuring sessions on healing the self, the community and the ecology.

**Important points**
- Organisers describe it as a movement to foster fungal literacy and reconnect people with nature, featuring sessions on healing the self, the community and the ecology.

#### Road closure: Mangaluru airport-bound travellers advised to take alternate routes

**In short:** Passengers travelling from Bajpe towards Mangaluru International Airport have been advised to take alternative routes following the temporary closure of a road near Karambaru after a small bridge near the Rego Fuel Station collapsed, prompting police to shut the stretch as a safety precaution. Airport authorities have advised passengers to follow police-suggested alternative routes and leave well ahead of scheduled travel time; travellers heading towards Mangaluru and the airport should use the Jarinnagara or Porkodi roads, as should motorists travelling towards Kateel, Bajpe and Kaikamba until normal traffic movement is restored.

#### I do not get works done through KRIDL, says Health Minister

**In short:** Health Minister U.T. Khader said none of the development works in his Mangaluru Assembly Constituency are executed by KRIDL, and works using rain damage grants were done by Panchayat Raj and PWD engineering wings.

Health Minister U.T. Khader said none of the development works in his Mangaluru Assembly Constituency are executed by KRIDL, and works using rain damage grants were done by Panchayat Raj and PWD engineering wings. Reacting to Eshwar Khandre ordering an inquiry into Puttur MLA Ashok Kumar Rai's corruption allegations against two KRIDL engineers of Dakshina Kannada, Khader said the inquiry will bring out the truth. Khader said raids on eateries, hotels, catering units, hostels and PG centres will continue, and that food safety norms regarding frozen "Kandy" fish were being prepared as he held a meeting with stakeholders to work out demands of Coastal Karnataka from the first Cabinet meeting of D.K. Shivakumar's cabinet in Mangaluru on September 18.

#### Traffic between Mangaluru and Bajpe affected as minor bridge becomes unstable

**In short:** Vehicular movement between Mangaluru and Bajpe on Mangaluru-Athradi State Highway 67 was affected after an old minor bridge built in 1963 at Karambar, between MIA and Bajpe, became unstable following the collapse of a portion of its arch on Wednesday. Traffic between the city and the airport was not affected as the damaged bridge lies beyond MIA; those travelling from Bajpe to the airport and on the city–Kateel pilgrim route were disturbed, and the district administration advised road users to use Porkodi–Jokatte to reach the city and Jarinagara to reach the airport.

Vehicular movement between Mangaluru and Bajpe on Mangaluru-Athradi State Highway 67 was affected after an old minor bridge built in 1963 at Karambar, between MIA and Bajpe, became unstable following the collapse of a portion of its arch on Wednesday. Traffic between the city and the airport was not affected as the damaged bridge lies beyond MIA; those travelling from Bajpe to the airport and on the city–Kateel pilgrim route were disturbed, and the district administration advised road users to use Porkodi–Jokatte to reach the city and Jarinagara to reach the airport. PWD engineers had sent reconstruction proposals years ago without government approval and will now send an emergency proposal; after temporary restoration, vehicles other than very heavy ones could ply until a new bridge is constructed.

**What this is**
- **Concept:** Traffic between Mangaluru and Bajpe affected as minor bridge becomes unstable
- **Told:** Vehicular movement between Mangaluru and Bajpe on Mangaluru-Athradi State Highway 67 was affected after an old minor bridge built in 1963 at Karambar, between MIA and Bajpe, became unstable following the collapse of a portion of its arch on Wednesday.
- **Purpose:** PWD engineers had sent reconstruction proposals years ago without government approval and will now send an emergency proposal; after temporary restoration, vehicles other than very heavy ones could ply until a new bridge is constructed.

**Important points**
- PWD engineers had sent reconstruction proposals years ago without government approval and will now send an emergency proposal.
- after temporary restoration, vehicles other than very heavy ones could ply until a new bridge is constructed.
_Sources: The Hindu Mangaluru p.3_

### Coastal Karnataka

#### Elaborate security arrangements made for Vitla Pindi in Udupi

**In short:** The district police have made elaborate security and traffic arrangements for the Vitla Pindi/Krishna Janmashtami celebrations in Udupi, deploying senior officers, 186 constables, 50 Home Guards, a KSRP platoon, nine DAR platoons, fire and emergency teams, ambulances and drone teams. Seven parking locations have been identified, CCTV cameras installed at 45 additional strategic locations, and fingerprint scanners and public announcement systems arranged to manage crowds and ensure the safety of women and children.

#### Three booked for scuffle near Puttur Press Club

**In short:** Puttur Town Police have registered a case against three persons and others for allegedly engaging in a scuffle and disturbing peace near the Puttur Press Club after Inspector Johnson D'Souza found several people assaulting each other despite police intervention. #### Boat capsizes in Gangolli estuary; fishermen flag safety concerns

**In short:** A fishing boat partially capsized in the Gangolli estuary on Wednesday but all six fishermen aboard Manjunatha Sagara were rescued by nearby purse seine boats; the vessel was later towed back to Gangolli harbour though nets and equipment were damaged. Fishermen raised concerns that a purse seine boat provided to the Coastal Security Police Station for emergencies remains non-functional a month into the fishing season.

**In short:** A 68-year-old tourist from Bengaluru, Venkatesh G S of Dasarahalli, drowned in the Arabian Sea near Ramatheertha at Gokarna's Main Beach on Wednesday morning; police suspect he slipped on rocks near the shore and his son, a software professional in Bengaluru, has been informed.

#### Three sub-inspectors suspended over bribe-for-post conversation

**In short:** Three sub-inspectors in Uttara Kannada district have been suspended after an audio recording surfaced on social media, allegedly featuring a discussion about money paid to secure the Haliyal law-and-order SI posting. In the roughly two-minute clip, the officers, who are friends, are purportedly heard discussing the alleged payment and being posted in the same taluk to make money together.

Three sub-inspectors in Uttara Kannada district have been suspended after an audio recording surfaced on social media, allegedly featuring a discussion about money paid to secure the Haliyal law-and-order SI posting. In the roughly two-minute clip, the officers, who are friends, are purportedly heard discussing the alleged payment and being posted in the same taluk to make money together. Uttara Kannada Superintendent of Police M N Deepan ordered the action and initiated a departmental enquiry; the recording has gone viral, with the JD(S) social media cell sharing it and accusing the ruling government of corruption regarding police postings.

**What this is**
- **Concept:** Three sub-inspectors suspended over bribe-for-post conversation

#### Puttur Congress infighting surfaces after clash between workers and MLA's supporters; cops register FIR

**In short:** Internal differences within the Congress in Dakshina Kannada's Puttur came to the fore following a clash between supporters of MLA Ashok Kumar Rai and Congress workers in front of Puttur Press Club, where a heated argument allegedly turned physical in the presence of Rai. The dispute was reportedly linked to an audio clip circulating on social media; Congress worker Hakeem Koornadka had arrived to speak to mediapersons when some of the MLA's supporters allegedly confronted and surrounded him.

Internal differences within the Congress in Dakshina Kannada's Puttur came to the fore following a clash between supporters of MLA Ashok Kumar Rai and Congress workers in front of Puttur Press Club, where a heated argument allegedly turned physical in the presence of Rai. The dispute was reportedly linked to an audio clip circulating on social media; Congress worker Hakeem Koornadka had arrived to speak to mediapersons when some of the MLA's supporters allegedly confronted and surrounded him. Puttur town police registered an FIR based on a complaint by Puttur inspector Johnson D'Souza, naming Praveen Acharya of Munduru, Rawoof of Koornadka, Hakeem of Bolwar and others under Section 194(2) of the Bharatiya Nyaya Sanhita for unlawful assembly and disturbance of public order.

**What this is**
- **Concept:** Puttur Congress infighting surfaces after clash between workers and MLA's supporters; cops register FIR
- **Told:** Internal differences within the Congress in Dakshina Kannada's Puttur came to the fore following a clash between supporters of MLA Ashok Kumar Rai and Congress workers in front of Puttur Press Club, where a heated argument allegedly turned physical in the presence of Rai.
- **Purpose:** Puttur town police registered an FIR based on a complaint by Puttur inspector Johnson D'Souza, naming Praveen Acharya of Munduru, Rawoof of Koornadka, Hakeem of Bolwar and others under Section 194(2) of the Bharatiya Nyaya Sanhita for unlawful assembly and disturbance of public order.

#### All for Jawans: 8 lakh Tulasi leaves to be offered for their welfare

**In short:** A special worship featuring eight lakh Tulasi leaves will be held at the Sri Swarnavalli Mahasamsthana in Sonda on September 4 to pray for the health, safety and well-being of Indian soldiers serving along the nation's borders, as part of Sri Krishna Janmashtami celebrations. The programme will be held in the presence of Sri Gangadharendra Saraswati Mahaswamiji, Jagadguru Shankaracharya of the Sri Swarnavalli Mahasamsthana, and Sri Anandabodhendra Saraswati Mahaswamiji; special pujas will conclude with Maha Mangalarati in the afternoon followed by distribution of Teertha Prasada.

A special worship featuring eight lakh Tulasi leaves will be held at the Sri Swarnavalli Mahasamsthana in Sonda on September 4 to pray for the health, safety and well-being of Indian soldiers serving along the nation's borders, as part of Sri Krishna Janmashtami celebrations. The programme will be held in the presence of Sri Gangadharendra Saraswati Mahaswamiji, Jagadguru Shankaracharya of the Sri Swarnavalli Mahasamsthana, and Sri Anandabodhendra Saraswati Mahaswamiji; special pujas will conclude with Maha Mangalarati in the afternoon followed by distribution of Teertha Prasada. The annual Tulasi worship was started during the Kargil War following a spiritual resolve by the pontiff and has entered its 27th year; devotees can collect Tulasi leaves from plants around their homes and bring them to the Swarnavalli Math on or before September 4.

#### Kinnigoli-Mulki highway offers a challenge: Travel on stretch without falling into a pothole!

**In short:** The Kinnigoli-Mulki state highway has deteriorated badly, with the asphalt surface completely worn out at several stretches and potholes dotting the road, making it almost impossible for commuters to travel without falling into a pothole. The poor condition has reportedly caused several motorists, particularly two-wheeler riders, to sustain injuries; work related to the Amrut-2 drinking water pipeline further worsened the situation as the road was cut at several places and allegedly not restored properly.

The Kinnigoli-Mulki state highway has deteriorated badly, with the asphalt surface completely worn out at several stretches and potholes dotting the road, making it almost impossible for commuters to travel without falling into a pothole. The poor condition has reportedly caused several motorists, particularly two-wheeler riders, to sustain injuries; work related to the Amrut-2 drinking water pipeline further worsened the situation as the road was cut at several places and allegedly not restored properly. Large potholes have developed at stretches including near S. Kodi, Sukananda Shetty Circle, the bus stand turn, Kinnigoli Rajangana, Hosakaveri Katte, Moorkaveri, Padmanuru and Kerekadu; residents said four people suffered injuries after falling from two-wheelers over the past two days, and Rs 2 crore proposed for development of the Mulki-Kinnigoli road has not yet been sanctioned.

**What this is**
- **Concept:** Kinnigoli-Mulki highway offers a challenge: Travel on stretch without falling into a pothole!
- **Told:** The Kinnigoli-Mulki state highway has deteriorated badly, with the asphalt surface completely worn out at several stretches and potholes dotting the road, making it almost impossible for commuters to travel without falling into a pothole.
- **Purpose:** Kodi, Sukananda Shetty Circle, the bus stand turn, Kinnigoli Rajangana, Hosakaveri Katte, Moorkaveri, Padmanuru and Kerekadu; residents said four people suffered injuries after falling from two-wheelers over the past two days, and Rs 2 crore proposed for development of the Mulki-Kinnigoli road has not yet been sanctioned.

**Important points**
- Large potholes have developed at stretches including near S.
- Kodi, Sukananda Shetty Circle, the bus stand turn, Kinnigoli Rajangana, Hosakaveri Katte, Moorkaveri, Padmanuru and Kerekadu.

#### Six fishermen rescued

**In short:** Six fishermen were rescued from a fishing boat that capsized in the Panchagangavali estuary near Gangolli Fishing Harbour in Udupi district after the boat got stuck in the water current in the estuary and started sinking. Fishermen in the vicinity rushed and saved the six; they pulled the boat to the harbour but valuables including the fishing net were lost.

#### Capt. Chowta thanks Ashwini Vaishnaw

**In short:** Dakshina Kannada MP Capt. Brijesh Chowta thanked Railway Minister Ashwini Vaishnaw on Tuesday for proactive direction in bringing the Mangaluru Railway Region under South Western Railway's Mysuru division.

Dakshina Kannada MP Capt. Brijesh Chowta thanked Railway Minister Ashwini Vaishnaw on Tuesday for proactive direction in bringing the Mangaluru Railway Region under South Western Railway's Mysuru division. The MP met Vaishnaw in New Delhi and expressed gratitude for the August 21 Railway Ministry order excluding the Mangaluru region from Southern Railway's Palakkad division and integrating it with SWR Mysuru division, fulfilling a decades-old demand of people from Karnataka, particularly coastal Karnataka, in an administrative change bound to bring greater coherence to railway administration of the region.

**What this is**
- **Concept:** Capt. Chowta thanks Ashwini Vaishnaw
- **Told:** Dakshina Kannada MP Capt.
- **Purpose:** The MP met Vaishnaw in New Delhi and expressed gratitude for the August 21 Railway Ministry order excluding the Mangaluru region from Southern Railway's Palakkad division and integrating it with SWR Mysuru division, fulfilling a decades-old demand of people from Karnataka, particularly coastal Karnataka, in an administrative change bound to bring greater coherence to railway administration of the region.

**Important points**
- The MP met Vaishnaw in New Delhi and expressed gratitude for the August 21 Railway Ministry order excluding the Mangaluru region from Southern Railway's Palakkad division and integrating it with SWR Mysuru division, fulfilling a decades-old demand of people from Karnataka, particularly coastal Karnataka, in an administrative change bound to bring greater coherence to railway administration of the region.
_Sources: The Hindu Mangaluru p.2_

### Karnataka

#### KSPC scam: CID traces money trail

**In short:** The Criminal Investigation Department, probing alleged Karnataka Public Service Commission irregularities, intensified its investigation to trace the money trail and found strong monetary leads linking the two key suspects currently in its custody, well-placed sources told DH. As many as 10 CID officers, led by a deputy superintendent of police, searched the Om Lakshmi Souharda Sahakari Sangha Niyamitha cooperative society in Lecture Colony, Bhalki in Bidar district on Wednesday; one key suspect, Basavaraj Kannale, is from Bhalki and is in CID custody along with IAS officer Gyanendra Kumar Gangwar, who served as Controller of Examinations at the KPSC from February 2024 to March 2026.

The Criminal Investigation Department, probing alleged Karnataka Public Service Commission irregularities, intensified its investigation to trace the money trail and found strong monetary leads linking the two key suspects currently in its custody, well-placed sources told DH. As many as 10 CID officers, led by a deputy superintendent of police, searched the Om Lakshmi Souharda Sahakari Sangha Niyamitha cooperative society in Lecture Colony, Bhalki in Bidar district on Wednesday; one key suspect, Basavaraj Kannale, is from Bhalki and is in CID custody along with IAS officer Gyanendra Kumar Gangwar, who served as Controller of Examinations at the KPSC from February 2024 to March 2026. Sources said an average of Rs 40 lakh was collected from each candidate and routed through this cooperative society, that Kannale had multiple accounts in relatives' names used to launder crime proceeds with Gangwar among the beneficiaries, and that among 29 candidates who allegedly paid lakhs for postings after the Veterinary Officers' Recruitment Exam is Sooguresh Jaka, said to be a relative of former KPSC chairman Shivashankarappa S Sahukar; another CID team conducted mahajars at the OMR strong room and KPSC office in Bengaluru.

**What this is**
- **Concept:** KSPC scam: CID traces money trail
- **Told:** The Criminal Investigation Department, probing alleged Karnataka Public Service Commission irregularities, intensified its investigation to trace the money trail and found strong monetary leads linking the two key suspects currently in its custody, well-placed sources told DH.
- **Purpose:** Sources said an average of Rs 40 lakh was collected from each candidate and routed through this cooperative society, that Kannale had multiple accounts in relatives' names used to launder crime proceeds with Gangwar among the beneficiaries, and that among 29 candidates who allegedly paid lakhs for postings after the Veterinary Officers' Recruitment Exam is Sooguresh Jaka, said to be a relative of former KPSC chairman Shivashankarappa S Sahukar; another CID team conducted mahajars at the OMR strong room and KPSC office in Bengaluru.

**Important points**
- Sources said an average of Rs 40 lakh was collected from each candidate and routed through this cooperative society, that Kannale had multiple accounts in relatives' names used to launder crime proceeds with Gangwar among the beneficiaries, and that among 29 candidates who allegedly paid lakhs for postings after the Veterinary Officers' Recruitment Exam is Sooguresh Jaka, said to be a relative of former KPSC chairman Shivashankarappa S Sahukar.
- another CID team conducted mahajars at the OMR strong room and KPSC office in Bengaluru.
_Sources: Deccan Herald Mangaluru p.1; Deccan Herald Mangaluru p.5; News Trail Mangaluru p.2_

#### SIR: Lay greater emphasis on including names, not deleting them, Khader urges EC

**In short:** Health and Family Welfare Minister U T Khader urged officials and the Election Commission to give greater emphasis to including eligible voters rather than concentrating only on deletion during the ongoing second phase of the Special Intensive Revision, saying every citizen who has attained 18 and is eligible should be on the electoral roll. He expressed concern that people working or residing abroad could be excluded because they cannot appear in person for verification and asked the EC to explore a suitable technical or digital verification mechanism.

Health and Family Welfare Minister U T Khader urged officials and the Election Commission to give greater emphasis to including eligible voters rather than concentrating only on deletion during the ongoing second phase of the Special Intensive Revision, saying every citizen who has attained 18 and is eligible should be on the electoral roll. He expressed concern that people working or residing abroad could be excluded because they cannot appear in person for verification and asked the EC to explore a suitable technical or digital verification mechanism. Khader also said food safety inspections would be intensified across Mangaluru, Hubballi, Belagavi, Mysuru and other parts of the state, announced a one-day Yuva Dasara near Thokkottu bus stand in Ullal, ordered an inquiry into KRIDL contractor harassment allegations, and said authorities were examining FSSAI rules on frozen fish sold along the coast during the fishing ban.

**What this is**
- **Concept:** SIR: Lay greater emphasis on including names, not deleting them, Khader urges EC
- **Told:** Health and Family Welfare Minister U T Khader urged officials and the Election Commission to give greater emphasis to including eligible voters rather than concentrating only on deletion during the ongoing second phase of the Special Intensive Revision, saying every citizen who has attained 18 and is eligible should be on the electoral roll.
- **Purpose:** Khader also said food safety inspections would be intensified across Mangaluru, Hubballi, Belagavi, Mysuru and other parts of the state, announced a one-day Yuva Dasara near Thokkottu bus stand in Ullal, ordered an inquiry into KRIDL contractor harassment allegations, and said authorities were examining FSSAI rules on frozen fish sold along the coast during the fishing ban.

**Important points**
- Khader also said food safety inspections would be intensified across Mangaluru.
- Hubballi.
- Belagavi.
- Mysuru and other parts of the state, announced a one-day Yuva Dasara near Thokkottu bus stand in Ullal, ordered an inquiry into KRIDL contractor harassment allegations, and said authorities were examining FSSAI rules on frozen fish sold along the coast during the fishing ban.

#### Study Abroad Expo 2026 on September 6

**In short:** The Karnataka government is organising the Study Abroad Expo 2026 at Bengaluru's Palace Grounds on September 6 with around 75 global universities participating, Minister U T Khader said, describing it as a platform to help students pursue overseas education through proper channels instead of middlemen. He also announced the Karnataka State Brahmin Development Board's Sandipani Scholarship Scheme offering Rs 15,000 per year to eligible First and Second PUC and degree students, with applications open on ksbdb.karnataka.gov.in until December 31, 2026.

#### NITI Aayog for 'second Bengaluru' to ease pressure on city

**In short:** Chief Minister Shivakumar said several ambitious projects are at the implementation stage to improve Bengaluru's infrastructure and appealed to NITI Aayog for special consideration for Karnataka, noting the nation's huge expectations from the state as a leader in GDP, per capita income, human development index, technology and innovation, foreign direct investment, exports, start-ups and IT services. He sought full support and requested that NITI Aayog ensure Karnataka gets its due share of Central funds, including pending grants from earlier Central Budgets.

He sought full support and requested that NITI Aayog ensure Karnataka gets its due share of Central funds, including pending grants from earlier Central Budgets. #### Nandi Hills ropeway: Panel for action on officials over violations

**In short:** A MoEF&CC expert committee deferred a decision on the Rs 93.4-crore Nandi Hills ropeway project and directed the Karnataka government to take action against officials responsible for unauthorised activities on 3.2 acres of forest land, rejecting offered compensatory afforestation lands as unsuitable and asking the state to reduce the number of trees proposed to be felled.

#### Juvenile held for posing as IAS officer

**In short:** A juvenile was apprehended for allegedly cheating people by creating fake Facebook accounts in the names of IAS and IPS officers to seek money, after a person was cheated through a fake account using Electronics City sub-division DCP Narayan's name and photograph. Police traced the fraud network to Kotakurd village in Rajasthan and took the juvenile into custody after serving notice to the parents on August 24.

A juvenile was apprehended for allegedly cheating people by creating fake Facebook accounts in the names of IAS and IPS officers to seek money, after a person was cheated through a fake account using Electronics City sub-division DCP Narayan's name and photograph. #### Tourism boost: State nod to 18 hotel projects worth ₹314.93 cr

**In short:** Karnataka has approved 18 hotel projects — eight premium and 10 budget — at the sixth Empowered Committee meeting of the Karnataka Tourism Policy, chaired by Energy and Tourism Minister K.J. George, to strengthen tourism infrastructure and attract private investment.

Karnataka has approved 18 hotel projects — eight premium and 10 budget — at the sixth Empowered Committee meeting of the Karnataka Tourism Policy, chaired by Energy and Tourism Minister K.J. George, to strengthen tourism infrastructure and attract private investment. The 18 projects are expected to bring ₹314.93 crore in investment, add 655 hotel rooms and create around 1,542 jobs, with subsidies of approximately ₹47.23 crore; the committee also accorded in-principle approval to 78 additional hotel projects worth ₹1,080.97 crore, 3,424 rooms and 3,707 employment opportunities. George said improving quality accommodation was a priority and that the policy aimed to encourage private investment while strengthening local economic activity across the state.

**What this is**
- **Concept:** Tourism boost: State nod to 18 hotel projects worth ₹314.93 cr
- **Told:** Karnataka has approved 18 hotel projects — eight premium and 10 budget — at the sixth Empowered Committee meeting of the Karnataka Tourism Policy, chaired by Energy and Tourism Minister K.J.
- **Purpose:** George said improving quality accommodation was a priority and that the policy aimed to encourage private investment while strengthening local economic activity across the state.

**Important points**
- The 18 projects are expected to bring ₹314.93 crore in investment, add 655 hotel rooms and create around 1,542 jobs, with subsidies of approximately ₹47.23 crore.
- the committee also accorded in-principle approval to 78 additional hotel projects worth ₹1,080.97 crore, 3,424 rooms and 3,707 employment opportunities.
- George said improving quality accommodation was a priority and that the policy aimed to encourage private investment while strengthening local economic activity across the state.
_Sources: Deccan Herald Mangaluru p.3; News Trail Mangaluru p.2_

#### HC to examine Governor's appeal on power to suspend KPSC chief

**In short:** The Karnataka High Court on Wednesday issued notice to suspended KPSC chairperson Shivashankarappa S. Sahukar on an appeal by the Special Secretary to the Governor challenging a single-judge ruling that the Governor could not suspend the chairperson without the aid and advice of the Council of Ministers and that post facto approval was invalid.

The Karnataka High Court on Wednesday issued notice to suspended KPSC chairperson Shivashankarappa S. Sahukar on an appeal by the Special Secretary to the Governor challenging a single-judge ruling that the Governor could not suspend the chairperson without the aid and advice of the Council of Ministers and that post facto approval was invalid. A Division Bench of Chief Justice Vibhu Bakhru and Justice K.S. Hemalekha adjourned the matter till December 2; the single judge on August 18 had set aside the Governor's July 10 suspension order while giving liberty to decide with the aid and advice of the Council.

- **Concept:** HC to examine Governor's appeal on power to suspend KPSC chief

- A Division Bench of Chief Justice Vibhu Bakhru and Justice K.S.

**In short:** Union Minister H.D. Kumaraswamy has questioned the accountability of Minister Priyank Kharge in connection with alleged irregularities in the recruitment of RDPR engineers, even as the KPSC veterinary officer recruitment scam continues to trigger political controversy.

Union Minister H.D. Kumaraswamy has questioned the accountability of Minister Priyank Kharge in connection with alleged irregularities in the recruitment of RDPR engineers, even as the KPSC veterinary officer recruitment scam continues to trigger political controversy. Kumaraswamy said he was not alleging Kharge committed wrongdoing but questioned how appointment orders for 24 people could have been issued without the RDPR minister knowing, and defended a former KPSC chairman whom Kharge had referred to as having supervised recruitment involving around 13,000 candidates with integrity. He demanded a fair investigation into alleged irregularities involving KAS and other examinations rather than a show investigation that is later closed.

#### No justice for Kargil war veteran? Land allotment delay drives him to suicide

**In short:** Retired serviceman Shivananda Reddy, who served in the Maratha Light Infantry and was injured during the Kargil War, allegedly consumed pesticide on August 28 at the Inspection Bungalow premises in Chintamani taluk, Chikkaballapura district, after a prolonged delay in allotting him land recommended in recognition of his military service. Reddy is undergoing treatment at a hospital in Bengaluru and warned in a letter that authorities would be responsible for any "untoward incidents" if his demand was not addressed.

#### FIR on woman for killing 2 daughters

**In short:** An FIR has been registered against 40-year-old Uma Devi for allegedly killing her four-month-old twin daughters before attempting to take her own life at their home in Vajarahalli, Bengaluru, on Tuesday. Devi, who married Ramalingappa in 2002 and gave birth to the twins on May 5, 2026, after IVF treatment, had shown a lack of interest in the children and abnormal behaviour about one-and-a-half months after delivery and was treated for postpartum depression at Raghavendra Hospital and NIMHANS; on September 1 Ramalingappa found her attempting to hang herself with a veil and rescued her, while the twins were found dead in the same room with doctors at Shankar Hospital suspecting drowning.

#### Bidar court issues injunction to stop child marriage

**In short:** An injunction has been issued by the court to prevent an individual child marriage in Bidar district, the District Child Protection Officer has stated. In connection with a child marriage scheduled to take place on Tuesday in a village in Humnabad taluk, the Judge of the Additional First Class Court, Humnabad, issued an injunction restraining the marriage under the guidance of the Deputy Commissioner and the Deputy Director, Department of Women and Child Development.

An injunction has been issued by the court to prevent an individual child marriage in Bidar district, the District Child Protection Officer has stated. In connection with a child marriage scheduled to take place on Tuesday in a village in Humnabad taluk, the Judge of the Additional First Class Court, Humnabad, issued an injunction restraining the marriage under the guidance of the Deputy Commissioner and the Deputy Director, Department of Women and Child Development. This is the first case in Bidar in which a court injunction has been obtained to prevent an individual child marriage; earlier, during a mass marriage programme in 2016–17, a court injunction had been obtained in one case.

**What this is**
- **Concept:** Bidar court issues injunction to stop child marriage
- **Told:** An injunction has been issued by the court to prevent an individual child marriage in Bidar district, the District Child Protection Officer has stated.

#### Food safety violations will face action: Khader

**In short:** Health and Family Welfare Minister U.T. Khader has warned hotels, restaurants, catering centres and PG operators against compromising food quality, saying violators of food safety laws will face stringent action irrespective of their connections.

Health and Family Welfare Minister U.T. Khader has warned hotels, restaurants, catering centres and PG operators against compromising food quality, saying violators of food safety laws will face stringent action irrespective of their connections. Addressing a media conference at the Circuit House, Khader said inspections would be expanded beyond Bengaluru to Mangaluru, Udupi, Hubballi, Belagavi and Mysuru, noting two establishments had already been shut in Bengaluru; QR and barcode stickers will be introduced to help consumers report violations. He urged PG and hostel operators to ensure hygienic, tasty and nutritious food for students, said the government was working towards NABL laboratories so adulteration test reports could withstand legal scrutiny, and ordered an inquiry into allegations raised in connection with food-related issues.

**What this is**
- **Concept:** Food safety violations will face action: Khader
- **Told:** Health and Family Welfare Minister U.T.

- Addressing a media conference at the Circuit House.
- Khader said inspections would be expanded beyond Bengaluru to Mangaluru.
- Udupi.

- Belagavi and Mysuru, noting two establishments had already been shut in Bengaluru.

#### Mysuru Dasara to witness coast's favourite Kambala for first time

**In short:** Coastal Karnataka's traditional buffalo race, Kambala, will make its official debut at the world-famous Mysuru Dasara this year, with more than 100 pairs of buffaloes from Dakshina Kannada and Udupi districts expected to participate on November 18 and 19. A joint meeting of senior office-bearers of the state and district Kambala associations and buffalo owners will be held on September 10 to finalise arrangements including transportation, accommodation and veterinary support; the Dasara committee will bear transportation expenses.

Coastal Karnataka's traditional buffalo race, Kambala, will make its official debut at the world-famous Mysuru Dasara this year, with more than 100 pairs of buffaloes from Dakshina Kannada and Udupi districts expected to participate on November 18 and 19. A joint meeting of senior office-bearers of the state and district Kambala associations and buffalo owners will be held on September 10 to finalise arrangements including transportation, accommodation and veterinary support; the Dasara committee will bear transportation expenses. Following directions from a high-level meeting chaired by Chief Minister D.K. Shivakumar, races will be conducted under the Tourism, Sports and Kannada and Culture departments, with the Nirmithi Kendra constructing a traditional Jodukare racing track at an estimated cost of ₹30 lakh to ₹40 lakh; a coastal food festival and cultural programmes are also planned.

**What this is**
- **Concept:** Mysuru Dasara to witness coast's favourite Kambala for first time
- **Told:** Coastal Karnataka's traditional buffalo race, Kambala, will make its official debut at the world-famous Mysuru Dasara this year, with more than 100 pairs of buffaloes from Dakshina Kannada and Udupi districts expected to participate on November 18 and 19.
- **Purpose:** Shivakumar, races will be conducted under the Tourism, Sports and Kannada and Culture departments, with the Nirmithi Kendra constructing a traditional Jodukare racing track at an estimated cost of ₹30 lakh to ₹40 lakh; a coastal food festival and cultural programmes are also planned.

**Important points**
- Following directions from a high-level meeting chaired by Chief Minister D.K.
- Shivakumar, races will be conducted under the Tourism, Sports and Kannada and Culture departments, with the Nirmithi Kendra constructing a traditional Jodukare racing track at an estimated cost of ₹30 lakh to ₹40 lakh.
- a coastal food festival and cultural programmes are also planned.
_Sources: News Trail Mangaluru p.3_

#### BJP's padayatra against corruption, failures of Cong govt enters day two

**In short:** Karnataka BJP's 'Ballari Chalo' padayatra to protest against alleged corruption and failures of the Congress government entered its second day on Wednesday, beginning at Santekellur in Maski and expected to cover about 22 km. Led by BJP state president B Y Vijayendra, the 10-day march that began at Lingasugur on Tuesday is scheduled to conclude in Ballari after covering approximately 185 km through Maski, Sindhanur, Karatagi, Gangavathi, Kampli, Sandur and Ballari Rural.

Karnataka BJP's 'Ballari Chalo' padayatra to protest against alleged corruption and failures of the Congress government entered its second day on Wednesday, beginning at Santekellur in Maski and expected to cover about 22 km. Led by BJP state president B Y Vijayendra, the 10-day march that began at Lingasugur on Tuesday is scheduled to conclude in Ballari after covering approximately 185 km through Maski, Sindhanur, Karatagi, Gangavathi, Kampli, Sandur and Ballari Rural. The BJP is protesting against alleged corruption, diversion of funds meant for Scheduled Castes and Scheduled Tribes, inadequate drought relief and neglect of North Karnataka, and will highlight issues concerning women, youth and farmers despite Planning Minister B Nagendra having resigned over Valmiki corporation embezzlement allegations.

**What this is**
- **Concept:** BJP's padayatra against corruption, failures of Cong govt enters day two
- **Told:** Karnataka BJP's 'Ballari Chalo' padayatra to protest against alleged corruption and failures of the Congress government entered its second day on Wednesday, beginning at Santekellur in Maski and expected to cover about 22 km.
- **Purpose:** The BJP is protesting against alleged corruption, diversion of funds meant for Scheduled Castes and Scheduled Tribes, inadequate drought relief and neglect of North Karnataka, and will highlight issues concerning women, youth and farmers despite Planning Minister B Nagendra having resigned over Valmiki corporation embezzlement allegations.

**Important points**
- The BJP is protesting against alleged corruption, diversion of funds meant for Scheduled Castes and Scheduled Tribes, inadequate drought relief and neglect of North Karnataka, and will highlight issues concerning women, youth and farmers despite Planning Minister B Nagendra having resigned over Valmiki corporation embezzlement allegations.
_Sources: News Trail Mangaluru p.5; News Trail Mangaluru p.5_

#### Sita's character greater than Rama's, says CM Shivakumar

**In short:** Karnataka Chief Minister D K Shivakumar on Wednesday praised women for their strength and sacrifices, saying Sita's character in the Ramayana was greater than Rama's, while inaugurating the fifth edition of 'Together We Grow-International Women Entrepreneurship Day' organised by the Ubuntu Consortium of Women Entrepreneurs Association in Bengaluru. He urged women entrepreneurs to take up leadership roles, highlighted the Congress government's five guarantee schemes including Shakti free bus travel and Gruha Lakshmi payments of Rs 2,000 each to 1.22 crore women, and cited Sonia Gandhi as an example of women's sacrifices.

Karnataka Chief Minister D K Shivakumar on Wednesday praised women for their strength and sacrifices, saying Sita's character in the Ramayana was greater than Rama's, while inaugurating the fifth edition of 'Together We Grow-International Women Entrepreneurship Day' organised by the Ubuntu Consortium of Women Entrepreneurs Association in Bengaluru. He urged women entrepreneurs to take up leadership roles, highlighted the Congress government's five guarantee schemes including Shakti free bus travel and Gruha Lakshmi payments of Rs 2,000 each to 1.22 crore women, and cited Sonia Gandhi as an example of women's sacrifices. Shivakumar pledged the government's full support to women entrepreneurs and called for moving away from the mindset that the first child should be a boy.

#### Cong holds protest condemning FIR against Rahul

**In short:** Karnataka Congress on Wednesday staged a protest at Freedom Park in Bengaluru, condemning the FIR registered against Leader of Opposition in the Lok Sabha Rahul Gandhi over his remarks on an alleged purification ritual held at the venue in Uttarakhand where AICC chief Mallikarjun Kharge addressed a rally. The protest under KPCC chief B K Hariprasad was attended by Chief Minister D K Shivakumar, former CM Siddaramaiah and other leaders, who claimed the FIR was registered due to pressure from BJP leaders in Uttarakhand and demanded its immediate withdrawal.

The protest under KPCC chief B K Hariprasad was attended by Chief Minister D K Shivakumar, former CM Siddaramaiah and other leaders, who claimed the FIR was registered due to pressure from BJP leaders in Uttarakhand and demanded its immediate withdrawal. Patil meeting AICC president M. Mallikarjun Kharge and AICC general secretary Randeep Singh Surjewala in New Delhi on Tuesday. #### Time Out survey: Bengaluru ranks 17th among world's top destinations for nightlife

**In short:** Bengaluru ranked 17th among the world's best 20 cities for nightlife in 2026 in a Time Out survey, ahead of Accra, Singapore and Mexico City and as the only Indian city on the list. Based on responses from more than 24,000 locals worldwide on quality, affordability, diversity, safety and community feel, plus votes from city editors, culture experts and nightlife specialists, Bengaluru scored 82% for locals saying it was easy to connect with people and 71% for nightlife quality, though affordability was weaker at 54%.

Bengaluru ranked 17th among the world's best 20 cities for nightlife in 2026 in a Time Out survey, ahead of Accra, Singapore and Mexico City and as the only Indian city on the list. Based on responses from more than 24,000 locals worldwide on quality, affordability, diversity, safety and community feel, plus votes from city editors, culture experts and nightlife specialists, Bengaluru scored 82% for locals saying it was easy to connect with people and 71% for nightlife quality, though affordability was weaker at 54%. Nightlife is expanding beyond Indiranagar, M.G. Road and Koramangala to Ulsoor, Rajajinagar and Whitefield; Bangkok topped the list, Berlin was second and Seoul 16th, and Chethan Hegde, owner of 1522, welcomed the recognition and hoped the government would frame policies supporting vibrant nightlife culture.

**What this is**
- **Concept:** Time Out survey: Bengaluru ranks 17th among world's top destinations for nightlife
- **Told:** Bengaluru ranked 17th among the world's best 20 cities for nightlife in 2026 in a Time Out survey, ahead of Accra, Singapore and Mexico City and as the only Indian city on the list.
- **Purpose:** Road and Koramangala to Ulsoor, Rajajinagar and Whitefield; Bangkok topped the list, Berlin was second and Seoul 16th, and Chethan Hegde, owner of 1522, welcomed the recognition and hoped the government would frame policies supporting vibrant nightlife culture.

**Important points**

- Road and Koramangala to Ulsoor, Rajajinagar and Whitefield.
- Bangkok topped the list, Berlin was second and Seoul 16th, and Chethan Hegde, owner of 1522, welcomed the recognition and hoped the government would frame policies supporting vibrant nightlife culture.

#### NIMHANS, prisons dept. sign MoU to strengthen inmates' mental healthcare

**In short:** The Department of Prisons and Correctional Services, Karnataka signed an MoU with NIMHANS Bengaluru for mental health support and research among incarcerated individuals. A multidisciplinary NIMHANS team will implement programmes addressing psychological and mental health concerns and strengthen the research wing of Central Prison Bengaluru, including evidence-based psychological support, psychometric assessments, psychotherapy, counselling and rehabilitative counselling.

The Department of Prisons and Correctional Services, Karnataka signed an MoU with NIMHANS Bengaluru for mental health support and research among incarcerated individuals. A multidisciplinary NIMHANS team will implement programmes addressing psychological and mental health concerns and strengthen the research wing of Central Prison Bengaluru, including evidence-based psychological support, psychometric assessments, psychotherapy, counselling and rehabilitative counselling. The partnership covers workshops on psychological well-being, awareness and sensitisation on mental health, batch-wise psychosocial training on suicide prevention and substance use perils, strengthening Tele-MANAS services, and facilitating communication and visits between inmates and families.

**What this is**
- **Concept:** NIMHANS, prisons dept. sign MoU to strengthen inmates' mental healthcare
- **Told:** The Department of Prisons and Correctional Services, Karnataka signed an MoU with NIMHANS Bengaluru for mental health support and research among incarcerated individuals.
- **Purpose:** The partnership covers workshops on psychological well-being, awareness and sensitisation on mental health, batch-wise psychosocial training on suicide prevention and substance use perils, strengthening Tele-MANAS services, and facilitating communication and visits between inmates and families.

**Important points**

#### SC/ST quota medical seats in State see a sharp decline

**In short:** Karnataka has reverted to its earlier reservation quantum for SCs and STs in government medical seats while cases against increased reservation in recruitment are pending in the High Court. SC reserved seats fell from 953 to 906 and ST seats from 392 to 182, out of total government seats rising from 5,609 in 2025-26 to 6,045 in 2026-27; after the 2022 Act set SC at 17% and ST at 7% from 2023-24, the share reverts to 15% and 3% from 2026-27.

Karnataka has reverted to its earlier reservation quantum for SCs and STs in government medical seats while cases against increased reservation in recruitment are pending in the High Court. SC reserved seats fell from 953 to 906 and ST seats from 392 to 182, out of total government seats rising from 5,609 in 2025-26 to 6,045 in 2026-27; after the 2022 Act set SC at 17% and ST at 7% from 2023-24, the share reverts to 15% and 3% from 2026-27. Community estimates suggest STs could lose about 2,800 posts and SCs about 1,400 in ongoing recruitment of roughly 72,000 vacancies, though the government says posts are kept in abeyance until a court ruling; Minister T. Raghumurthy acknowledged the decline and Devaraj T. Katur of Karnataka Rajya Nayakara Yuva Sene is fighting the reduction.

**What this is**
- **Concept:** SC/ST quota medical seats in State see a sharp decline
- **Told:** Karnataka has reverted to its earlier reservation quantum for SCs and STs in government medical seats while cases against increased reservation in recruitment are pending in the High Court.
- **Purpose:** Katur of Karnataka Rajya Nayakara Yuva Sene is fighting the reduction.

**Important points**
- Community estimates suggest STs could lose about 2,800 posts and SCs about 1,400 in ongoing recruitment of roughly 72,000 vacancies, though the government says posts are kept in abeyance until a court ruling.
- Minister T.
- Raghumurthy acknowledged the decline and Devaraj T.
- Katur of Karnataka Rajya Nayakara Yuva Sene is fighting the reduction.
_Sources: The Hindu Mangaluru p.4_

#### Op Sindoor ace is Ram Temple Trust CEO

**In short:** Air Marshal (retd) Jeetendra Mishra, who played a key role in air operations during Operation Sindoor, was on Wednesday appointed the first chief executive officer of the Shri Ram Janmabhoomi Teerth Kshetra Trust at a meeting of Trust members in Ayodhya. Mahant Govind Dev Giri was appointed the Trust's new general secretary, Madan Mohan Pandy, Mahesh Bhagchandka and Nirmala Yadav were appointed new members, and Bhagchandka was made treasurer.

Air Marshal (retd) Jeetendra Mishra, who played a key role in air operations during Operation Sindoor, was on Wednesday appointed the first chief executive officer of the Shri Ram Janmabhoomi Teerth Kshetra Trust at a meeting of Trust members in Ayodhya. Mahant Govind Dev Giri was appointed the Trust's new general secretary, Madan Mohan Pandy, Mahesh Bhagchandka and Nirmala Yadav were appointed new members, and Bhagchandka was made treasurer. Mishra, a resident of Uttar Pradesh's Deoria district and a decorated Indian Air Force fighter pilot, was among the three persons recommended by a search committee formed for selection of the CEO.

#### SC terms bar council chief's tenure 'pro tem'

**In short:** The Supreme Court on Wednesday described Bar Council of India Chairperson Manan Kumar Mishra's position as only "pro tem" until a newly constituted body elects its office-bearers and directed that every policy decision of the council must involve the Attorney General and the Solicitor General. A bench of Chief Justice of India Surya Kant and Justices Joymalya Bagchi and V Mohana, hearing petitions challenging the legality of Mishra's prolonged tenure and notifications extending the terms of the chairman and vice-chairman until 2030, said it was concerned solely with the institutional functioning of the BCI and not with the conduct of any individual.

The Supreme Court on Wednesday described Bar Council of India Chairperson Manan Kumar Mishra's position as only "pro tem" until a newly constituted body elects its office-bearers and directed that every policy decision of the council must involve the Attorney General and the Solicitor General. A bench of Chief Justice of India Surya Kant and Justices Joymalya Bagchi and V Mohana, hearing petitions challenging the legality of Mishra's prolonged tenure and notifications extending the terms of the chairman and vice-chairman until 2030, said it was concerned solely with the institutional functioning of the BCI and not with the conduct of any individual. Senior advocate Madhavi Divan pointed to a notification granting Mishra a five-year tenure from April 17, 2025, noting that a resolution had extended the term from three years to five years even though the BCI Rules prescribe only two years.

**What this is**
- **Concept:** SC terms bar council chief's tenure 'pro tem'
- **Told:** The Supreme Court on Wednesday described Bar Council of India Chairperson Manan Kumar Mishra's position as only "pro tem" until a newly constituted body elects its office-bearers and directed that every policy decision of the council must involve the Attorney General and the Solicitor General.
- **Purpose:** Senior advocate Madhavi Divan pointed to a notification granting Mishra a five-year tenure from April 17, 2025, noting that a resolution had extended the term from three years to five years even though the BCI Rules prescribe only two years.

**Important points**
- Senior advocate Madhavi Divan pointed to a notification granting Mishra a five-year tenure from April 17, 2025, noting that a resolution had extended the term from three years to five years even though the BCI Rules prescribe only two years.
_Sources: Deccan Herald Mangaluru p.1; Deccan Herald Mangaluru p.5; The Hindu Mangaluru p.1; The Hindu Mangaluru p.8_

#### HC dismisses plea challenging Rahul Gandhi's citizenship

**In short:** The Lucknow Bench of the Allahabad High Court dismissed a petition challenging Rahul Gandhi's citizenship and Lok Sabha membership after petitioner Ashok Pandey failed to produce documentary evidence that he holds British nationality; the court permitted him to withdraw the writ petition on August 31. Mr.

The Lucknow Bench of the Allahabad High Court dismissed a petition challenging Rahul Gandhi's citizenship and Lok Sabha membership after petitioner Ashok Pandey failed to produce documentary evidence that he holds British nationality; the court permitted him to withdraw the writ petition on August 31. Mr. Pandey had alleged Mr. Gandhi incorporated U.K.-based Backops Limited in 2003 and declared himself director, major shareholder, and a British national, but could not show company formation records or any British citizenship declaration when asked.

**What this is**
- **Concept:** HC dismisses plea challenging Rahul Gandhi's citizenship
- **Told:** The Lucknow Bench of the Allahabad High Court dismissed a petition challenging Rahul Gandhi's citizenship and Lok Sabha membership after petitioner Ashok Pandey failed to produce documentary evidence that he holds British nationality; the court permitted him to withdraw the writ petition on August 31.
- **Purpose:** Gandhi incorporated U.K.-based Backops Limited in 2003 and declared himself director, major shareholder, and a British national, but could not show company formation records or any British citizenship declaration when asked.

**Important points**
- Pandey had alleged Mr.
- Gandhi incorporated U.K.-based Backops Limited in 2003 and declared himself director, major shareholder, and a British national, but could not show company formation records or any British citizenship declaration when asked.

#### Rahul’s ‘Chhatron Ki Goonj’ event in Indore postponed, nod denied

**In short:** The Congress on Wednesday said Rahul Gandhi’s ‘Chhatron Ki Goonj’ event in Indore, planned for September 12, has been postponed to September 19 after the Madhya Pradesh government denied permission to use Jawaharlal Nehru Stadium, state Congress president Jitu Patwari announced. Patwari said the party will find a new venue, and had earlier written to Chief Minister Mohan Yadav requesting the stadium for what he described as a public dialogue on the country’s future, youth dreams, the right to education and education system security rather than a typical political event.

#### BJP agents' bulk deletion bid targets minority voters in J'khand

**In short:** A voter-roll revision in Jharkhand's Godda district has triggered confrontation after BJP Booth Level Agents filed stacks of Form 7 applications seeking deletion of voters, with most names from the minority community, and Booth Level Officers at at least four booths raised objections. At Booth No.

A voter-roll revision in Jharkhand's Godda district has triggered confrontation after BJP Booth Level Agents filed stacks of Form 7 applications seeking deletion of voters, with most names from the minority community, and Booth Level Officers at at least four booths raised objections. At Booth No. 9 in Maheshtikri village, BJP BLA Sanjeev Kumar Roshan filed 25 Form 7 applications against Muslim voters; with the claims and objections window closing on September 4, the ERO's verification will decide whether bulk deletion requests in Godda are upheld or rejected.

#### 7.8% or 2.6% GROWTH FIGURES REAL OR BOGUS?

**In short:** Former RBI Governor Raghuram Rajan has questioned whether India's 7.8 per cent GDP growth for Q1 FY27 adequately reflects ground realities, a day after MoSPI reported the figure, significantly above the RBI's 7 per cent estimate. Former Finance Secretary Subhash Chandra Garg argued growth would have been around 2.6 per cent if the previous year's GDP had not been revised down from around ₹86 lakh crore to ₹80 lakh crore—a calculation Statistics Secretary Saurabh Garg rejected as an "apples and oranges" exercise mixing two different GDP series.

#### Maha BJP accuses Congress of opposing women's reservation

**In short:** BJP leaders protested in Nashik on Wednesday against Congress MP Rahul Gandhi's visit, accusing his party of opposing women's representation in Parliament and state assemblies. Nashik BJP MLA Devayani Farande and Mayor Himgauri Aher-Adake led a protest march in black sarees with "Go back" slogans, citing the defeat in April of a Constitution Amendment Bill intended to provide 33 per cent reservation for women in the Lok Sabha and state assemblies from 2029.

#### Andhra literacy rate up to 82% from 72% in 2 years: Minister

**In short:** Andhra Pradesh Education Minister Nara Lokesh said on Wednesday that the state's literacy rate rose from 72.6 per cent in 2024 to 82.1 per cent in 2026, describing it as a "truly historic" transformation under the Akshara Andhra mission launched by the NDA government in 2025. Lokesh said Andhra Pradesh had one of the lowest literacy rates when he took charge in 2024, below even Bihar at 74.3 per cent, but moved above the national average of 81 per cent in two years, with around 25 lakh people becoming literate through the initiative.

Andhra Pradesh Education Minister Nara Lokesh said on Wednesday that the state's literacy rate rose from 72.6 per cent in 2024 to 82.1 per cent in 2026, describing it as a "truly historic" transformation under the Akshara Andhra mission launched by the NDA government in 2025. Lokesh said Andhra Pradesh had one of the lowest literacy rates when he took charge in 2024, below even Bihar at 74.3 per cent, but moved above the national average of 81 per cent in two years, with around 25 lakh people becoming literate through the initiative. He directed officials to work in mission mode to achieve 100 per cent literacy by September 2027 and prepared a phased plan to make another 25 lakh people literate by March 2027.

**What this is**
- **Concept:** Andhra literacy rate up to 82% from 72% in 2 years: Minister
- **Told:** Andhra Pradesh Education Minister Nara Lokesh said on Wednesday that the state's literacy rate rose from 72.6 per cent in 2024 to 82.1 per cent in 2026, describing it as a "truly historic" transformation under the Akshara Andhra mission launched by the NDA government in 2025.
- **Purpose:** He directed officials to work in mission mode to achieve 100 per cent literacy by September 2027 and prepared a phased plan to make another 25 lakh people literate by March 2027.

#### Andhra CM directs TTD, Endowments officials to expedite construction of 5,000 temples

**In short:** Andhra Pradesh Chief Minister N Chandrababu Naidu directed TTD and Endowments Department officials to expedite construction of 5,000 temples across the state and finalise sites at the earliest, an official release said late on Tuesday. TTD Executive Officer Muddada Ravi Chandra told a review meeting that proposals had been received for 4,192 temples from 161 constituencies in 28 districts, while 967 temples had already been completed, with priority for SC and ST colonies, fishermen settlements, weaker sections' colonies and general areas.

Andhra Pradesh Chief Minister N Chandrababu Naidu directed TTD and Endowments Department officials to expedite construction of 5,000 temples across the state and finalise sites at the earliest, an official release said late on Tuesday. TTD Executive Officer Muddada Ravi Chandra told a review meeting that proposals had been received for 4,192 temples from 161 constituencies in 28 districts, while 967 temples had already been completed, with priority for SC and ST colonies, fishermen settlements, weaker sections' colonies and general areas. Naidu suggested using Srivani Trust funds, utilising 11.8 lakh Srivari Seva volunteers at new temples, constructing Lord Venkateswara temples in other state capitals, restoring 1,000 ancient temples, and conducting special courses to prepare priests for idol installation.

#### 787 riders fined Rs 7.87 lakh over helmet

**In short:** Police in Maharashtra's Latur district launched a helmet enforcement drive on Tuesday, booking 787 two-wheeler riders for riding without protective headgear and collecting Rs 7.87 lakh in fines on the first day. Officials said awareness campaigns on helmet use had been conducted since March, but compliance remained below expectations, prompting strict enforcement from September 1, and added that the drive was not aimed at collecting fines.

#### DC urges Jarange to end fast as doctors flag health concerns on day five of protest

**In short:** Activist Manoj Jarange's hunger strike over the Maratha reservation issue entered its fifth day on Wednesday in Jalna district, as doctors raised concerns over his deteriorating health and Jalna district collector Ashima Mittal appealed to him to end his fast. A medical officer said Jarange's blood sugar level had dropped, and doctors camping at the protest site urged him to take IV fluids, which he refused.

Activist Manoj Jarange's hunger strike over the Maratha reservation issue entered its fifth day on Wednesday in Jalna district, as doctors raised concerns over his deteriorating health and Jalna district collector Ashima Mittal appealed to him to end his fast. A medical officer said Jarange's blood sugar level had dropped, and doctors camping at the protest site urged him to take IV fluids, which he refused. Jarange began his indefinite hunger strike at Antarwali Sarati on August 29, demanding reservation for Marathas and immediate issuance of Kunbi certificates on the basis of gazette records, and has threatened to intensify the agitation and take it to Mumbai if demands are not met. Mittal met Jarange on Tuesday night to explain government steps on Maratha reservation, but he questioned whether she had come to defend the government's position.

#### CM Naidu releases Godavari water to North Andhra

**In short:** Andhra Pradesh Chief Minister N Chandrababu Naidu on Wednesday released Godavari waters to North Andhra through the Polavaram Project Left Main Canal, marking a major step towards strengthening irrigation and drinking water supply in the region. The Chief Minister performed 'Jala Harathi' at Payakaraopeta in Anakapalli district and switched on the regulator, allowing water to flow through canals, rivers, aqueducts and syphons via the Pampa, Tandava, Varaha and Sarada rivers and the Yeleru canal.

Andhra Pradesh Chief Minister N Chandrababu Naidu on Wednesday released Godavari waters to North Andhra through the Polavaram Project Left Main Canal, marking a major step towards strengthening irrigation and drinking water supply in the region. The Chief Minister performed 'Jala Harathi' at Payakaraopeta in Anakapalli district and switched on the regulator, allowing water to flow through canals, rivers, aqueducts and syphons via the Pampa, Tandava, Varaha and Sarada rivers and the Yeleru canal. The Rs 4,192-crore canal, constructed over 213 km, will initially provide irrigation to 2.87 lakh acres in four districts and drinking water to around 5.23 lakh people, with specialised structures including a 447-metre aqueduct across the Tandava river designed to carry up to 10,634 cusecs of water.

**What this is**
- **Concept:** CM Naidu releases Godavari water to North Andhra
- **Told:** Andhra Pradesh Chief Minister N Chandrababu Naidu on Wednesday released Godavari waters to North Andhra through the Polavaram Project Left Main Canal, marking a major step towards strengthening irrigation and drinking water supply in the region.
- **Purpose:** The Rs 4,192-crore canal, constructed over 213 km, will initially provide irrigation to 2.87 lakh acres in four districts and drinking water to around 5.23 lakh people, with specialised structures including a 447-metre aqueduct across the Tandava river designed to carry up to 10,634 cusecs of water.

**Important points**
- The Rs 4,192-crore canal, constructed over 213 km, will initially provide irrigation to 2.87 lakh acres in four districts and drinking water to around 5.23 lakh people, with specialised structures including a 447-metre aqueduct across the Tandava river designed to carry up to 10,634 cusecs of water.
_Sources: News Trail Mangaluru p.4_

#### 'Handing hostels to RSS-linked trust'

**In short:** A tribal rights group has launched an agitation in Nagpur, alleging that the Maharashtra government has handed over the operations of nearly 500 tribal hostels to an "RSS-linked" trust, a claim officials rejected as "baseless." The Adivasi Adhikar Bachhav Kriti Samiti, which started its protest on Monday, said it would stage a peaceful demonstration at the RSS headquarters in Nagpur if the government did not revoke the decision. Co-coordinator Rajat Maheshgawli claimed the government has "fully handed over" 490 tribal hostels to the Late Laxmanrao Mankar Memorial Trust under an MoU with the Tribal Development Department, calling it a conspiracy to privatise tribal hostels and saying arrangements for tribal students' education, nutrition, accommodation, safety and basic rights must be free from interference.

#### Hyderabad SIR deletions exceed 2023 winning margins in all 15 constituencies, finds report

**In short:** Deletions in draft electoral rolls across 15 Hyderabad Assembly constituencies under the Special Intensive Revision (SIR) exceed the 2023 winning margin in every constituency, according to an analysis by Kolkata-based Sabar Institute. Muslim electors account for 40.7% of the 19,41,444 deletions in Hyderabad, ranging from 12.1% in Secunderabad Cantonment to 85.9% in Bahadurpura; in Malakpet and Yakutpura deletions exceed total votes polled in 2023.

Deletions in draft electoral rolls across 15 Hyderabad Assembly constituencies under the Special Intensive Revision (SIR) exceed the 2023 winning margin in every constituency, according to an analysis by Kolkata-based Sabar Institute. Muslim electors account for 40.7% of the 19,41,444 deletions in Hyderabad, ranging from 12.1% in Secunderabad Cantonment to 85.9% in Bahadurpura; in Malakpet and Yakutpura deletions exceed total votes polled in 2023. Statewide, 73.39 lakh voters were deleted from Telangana’s 17 August draft roll—21.3% of the electorate—with ECI officials noting the exercise is ongoing and comparing 2023 absolute voter numbers with ASDD figures frozen on 12 May.

**What this is**
- **Concept:** Hyderabad SIR deletions exceed 2023 winning margins in all 15 constituencies, finds report
- **Told:** Deletions in draft electoral rolls across 15 Hyderabad Assembly constituencies under the Special Intensive Revision (SIR) exceed the 2023 winning margin in every constituency, according to an analysis by Kolkata-based Sabar Institute.
- **Purpose:** Statewide, 73.39 lakh voters were deleted from Telangana’s 17 August draft roll—21.3% of the electorate—with ECI officials noting the exercise is ongoing and comparing 2023 absolute voter numbers with ASDD figures frozen on 12 May.

**Important points**
- Statewide, 73.39 lakh voters were deleted from Telangana’s 17 August draft roll—21.3% of the electorate—with ECI officials noting the exercise is ongoing and comparing 2023 absolute voter numbers with ASDD figures frozen on 12 May.

#### NESt Fest to showcase Northeast’s culture, youth talent in Delhi on September 4-5

**In short:** The ninth edition of the North East Students’ Festival (NESt Fest) will be held in New Delhi on September 4 and 5, showcasing the region’s culture, student talent, music, handicrafts, cuisine and youth voices. Organised by NGO My Home India at Talkatora Indoor Stadium under the theme “The Sound of India’s Yuva Shakti,” the free festival will open with an inaugural ceremony, cultural parade, government pavilion, folk performances and Janajatiya handicrafts and cuisine, with Delhi Chief Minister Rekha Gupta, Union Minister Jyotiraditya Scindia and several other leaders expected; the second day will feature a “Vande Mataram” inter-college competition, regional attire fashion show, youth pledge and valedictory session, with Haryana Chief Minister Nayab Singh Saini and Mizoram Governor V K Singh scheduled to participate.

#### Death toll 14: House of UP’s cracker factory blast accused razed

**In short:** Authorities have razed a three-storey house of main accused Naushad in the illegal cracker factory blast in Manouri town, Kaushambi, as the death toll rose to 14 after Urvashi (17) succumbed to injuries at SRN hospital in Prayagraj on Wednesday. Police raided a warehouse owned by his wife Kahkashan Bano, seized six sacks of firecrackers, demolished Naushad’s unauthorised house in an 11-hour operation, arrested Naushad along with Shabana and Saina, and are hunting three others with Rs 25,000 rewards each while collecting evidence to invoke the NSA; nine police personnel were suspended for alleged negligence after the August 31 blast killed 11 children and damaged houses within about 100 metres, with three properties collapsing completely.

#### Mettur dam cost Rs 4.8 cr, but Vijay’s visit cost Rs 5 cr: TN BJP chief

**In short:** BJP leader Nainar Nagenthran claimed on Wednesday that the Tamil Nadu administration spent Rs 5 crore on Chief Minister C Joseph Vijay’s recent visit to Mettur Dam, alleging the travel expenditure exceeded the Rs 4.80 crore historical cost of constructing the reservoir itself. Terming Vijay a “costly Chief Minister,” Nagendran questioned deploying barricades and spending public funds when the dam held only partial capacity, and accused the state government of failing delta farmers as Mettur’s sluice gates, traditionally opened on June 12, faced delays due to acute water shortage.
